   125	
   126	
   127	def compute_pred_loss(pred: torch.Tensor, target: torch.Tensor, cfg) -> torch.Tensor:
   128	    pred_type = cfg.loss.pred.get("type", "cosine")
   129	    return compute_embedding_loss(pred, target, loss_type=pred_type)
   130	
   131	
   132	def compute_multistep_rollout_loss(output, *, model, cfg):
   133	    rollout_cfg = cfg.loss.get("rollout", {})
   134	    rollout_steps = int(rollout_cfg.get("steps", 1))
   135	    if rollout_cfg.get("weight", 0.0) <= 0.0 or rollout_steps <= 1:
   136	        return None
   137	
   138	    ctx_len = cfg.wm.history_size
   139	    available_future = output["emb"].size(1) - ctx_len
   140	    rollout_steps = min(rollout_steps, available_future)
   141	    if rollout_steps <= 1:
   142	        return None
   143	
   144	    context_space = rollout_cfg.get("context_space", cfg.loss.pred.get("context_space", cfg.loss.pred.get("space", "normalized")))
   145	    target_space = rollout_cfg.get("space", cfg.loss.pred.get("space", "normalized"))
   146	    loss_type = rollout_cfg.get("type", cfg.loss.pred.get("type", "cosine"))
   147	
   148	    full_act_emb = output["act_emb"][:, : ctx_len + rollout_steps]
   149	    rollout_raw = output["emb_raw"][:, :ctx_len].clone()
   150	    rollout_norm = output["emb"][:, :ctx_len].clone()
   151	    target = get_embedding_tensor(output, space=target_space)[:, ctx_len : ctx_len + rollout_steps]
   152	
   153	    pred_steps = []
   154	    for step in range(rollout_steps):
   155	        action_end = ctx_len + step
   156	        window = min(ctx_len, action_end)
   157	        rollout_ctx = get_embedding_tensor(
   158	            {"emb_raw": rollout_raw, "emb": rollout_norm},
   159	            space=context_space,
   160	        )[:, -window:]
   161	        act_trunc = full_act_emb[:, action_end - window : action_end]
   162	
   163	        pred_raw = model.predict_raw(rollout_ctx, act_trunc)[:, -1:]
   164	        pred_norm = model.normalize_embeddings(pred_raw)
   165	        pred_steps.append(
   166	            get_embedding_tensor({"emb_raw": pred_raw, "emb": pred_norm}, space=target_space)
   167	        )
   168	
   169	        rollout_raw = torch.cat([rollout_raw, pred_raw], dim=1)
   170	        rollout_norm = torch.cat([rollout_norm, pred_norm], dim=1)
   171	
   172	    if len(pred_steps) <= 1:
   173	        return None
   174	
   175	    # Keep the first rollout step only as autoregressive context so this
   176	    # auxiliary term supervises strictly beyond the one-step pred_loss.
   177	    pred = torch.cat(pred_steps[1:], dim=1)
   178	    target = target[:, 1:]
   179	    return compute_embedding_loss(pred, target, loss_type=loss_type)
   180	
   181	
   182	def swm_forward(self, batch, stage, cfg):
   183	    """Encode observations, predict next states, compute spherical losses.
   184	
   185	    Losses:
   186	      pred_loss   — configurable prediction loss in raw or normalized space
   187	      reg_loss    — configurable anti-collapse loss in raw or normalized space
   188	      temporal_hinge_loss — optional hinge loss on consecutive latent pairs
   189	      loss        — pred_loss + λ_reg * reg_loss + λ_hinge * temporal_hinge_loss
   190	
   191	    Note:
   192	      temporal_straightness is logged as a monitoring metric using Euclidean
   193	      displacement vectors between consecutive unit-norm latents. On the sphere
   194	      this is only a chord-space approximation, so it may not reflect true
   195	      geodesic straightening accurately.
   196	    """
   197	    ctx_len = cfg.wm.history_size
   198	    n_preds = cfg.wm.num_preds
   199	    reg_type = cfg.loss.regularizer.type
   200	    reg_lambd = cfg.loss.regularizer.weight
   201	    hinge_cfg = cfg.loss.temporal_hinge
   202	    reg_space = cfg.loss.regularizer.get("space", "normalized")
   203	    pred_space = cfg.loss.pred.get("space", "normalized")
   204	    context_space = cfg.loss.pred.get("context_space", pred_space)
   205	
   206	    # Replace NaN values with 0 (occurs at sequence boundaries)
   207	    batch["action"] = torch.nan_to_num(batch["action"], 0.0)
   208	
   209	    output = self.model.encode(batch)
   210	
   211	    emb = output["emb"]  # (B, T, D), unit vectors
   212	    act_emb = output["act_emb"]
   213	
   214	    # Make training-time autoregressive context explicit so raw-consistent
   215	    # experiments can feed predictor history from the same space used for the
   216	    # prediction target and planning rollout.
   217	    ctx_emb = get_context_tensor(output, space=context_space)[:, :ctx_len]
   218	    ctx_act = act_emb[:, :ctx_len]
   219	
   220	    pred_raw = self.model.predict_raw(ctx_emb, ctx_act)
   221	    pred_emb = self.model.normalize_embeddings(pred_raw)
   222	
   223	    pred_source, tgt_source = get_loss_space_tensors(
   224	        output,
   225	        pred_raw=pred_raw,
   226	        pred_norm=pred_emb,
   227	        n_preds=n_preds,
   228	        space=pred_space,
   229	    )
   230	    output["pred_loss"] = compute_pred_loss(pred_source, tgt_source, cfg)
   231	    output["rollout_loss"] = compute_multistep_rollout_loss(
   232	        output,
   233	        model=self.model,
   234	        cfg=cfg,
   235	    )
   236	
   237	    reg_emb = get_regularizer_tensor(output, space=reg_space)
   238	    reg_scope = cfg.loss.regularizer.get("scope", "full").lower()
   239	    if reg_scope == "pred_window":
   240	        reg_emb = reg_emb[:, : ctx_len + n_preds]
   241	    elif reg_scope != "full":
   242	        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss.regularizer.scope: {reg_scope}")
   243	    if reg_type == "spread":
   244	        output["spread_loss"] = spread_loss(reg_emb, cfg.loss.spread.margin)
   245	        output["reg_loss"] = output["spread_loss"]
   246	    elif reg_type == "uniformity":
   247	        output["uniformity_loss"] = uniformity_loss(
   248	            reg_emb,
   249	            cfg.loss.uniformity.t,
   250	            mode=cfg.loss.uniformity.get("mode", "all_pairs"),
   251	            temporal_exclusion=cfg.loss.uniformity.get("temporal_exclusion", 0),
   252	        )
   253	        output["reg_loss"] = output["uniformity_loss"]
   254	    elif reg_type == "infonce":
   255	        tgt_emb = output["emb"][:, n_preds:]
   256	        output["infonce_loss"] = infonce_loss(
   257	            pred_emb, tgt_emb, cfg.loss.infonce.temperature
   258	        )
   259	        output["reg_loss"] = output["infonce_loss"]
   260	    else:

Output:
import os

os.environ["MUJOCO_GL"] = "egl"

import time
from pathlib import Path

import hydra
import numpy as np
import stable_pretraining as spt
import torch
from omegaconf import DictConfig, OmegaConf
from sklearn import preprocessing
from torchvision.transforms import v2 as transforms
import stable_worldmodel as swm


def img_transform(cfg):
    transform = transforms.Compose(
        [
            transforms.ToImage(),
            transforms.ToDtype(torch.float32, scale=True),
            transforms.Normalize(**spt.data.dataset_stats.ImageNet),
            transforms.Resize(size=cfg.eval.img_size),
        ]
    )
    return transform


def get_episodes_length(dataset, episodes):
    col_name = "episode_idx" if "episode_idx" in dataset.column_names else "ep_idx"

    episode_idx = dataset.get_col_data(col_name)
    step_idx = dataset.get_col_data("step_idx")
    lengths = []
    for ep_id in episodes:
        lengths.append(np.max(step_idx[episode_idx == ep_id]) + 1)
    return np.array(lengths)


def get_dataset(cfg, dataset_name):
    dataset_path = Path(cfg.cache_dir or swm.data.utils.get_cache_dir())
    dataset = swm.data.HDF5Dataset(
        dataset_name,
        keys_to_cache=cfg.dataset.keys_to_cache,
        cache_dir=dataset_path,
    )
    return dataset


@hydra.main(version_base=None, config_path="./config/eval", config_name="pusht")
def run(cfg: DictConfig):
    """Run evaluation of dinowm vs random policy."""
    assert (
        cfg.plan_config.horizon * cfg.plan_config.action_block <= cfg.eval.eval_budget
    ), "Planning horizon must be smaller than or equal to eval_budget"

    # create world environment
    cfg.world.max_episode_steps = 2 * cfg.eval.eval_budget
    world = swm.World(**cfg.world, image_shape=(224, 224))

    # create the transform
    transform = {
        "pixels": img_transform(cfg),
        "goal": img_transform(cfg),
    }

    dataset = get_dataset(cfg, cfg.eval.dataset_name)
    stats_dataset = dataset  # get_dataset(cfg, cfg.dataset.stats)
    col_name = "episode_idx" if "episode_idx" in dataset.column_names else "ep_idx"
    ep_indices, _ = np.unique(stats_dataset.get_col_data(col_name), return_index=True)

    process = {}
    for col in cfg.dataset.keys_to_cache:
        if col in ["pixels"]:
            continue
        processor = preprocessing.StandardScaler()
        col_data = stats_dataset.get_col_data(col)
        col_data = col_data[~np.isnan(col_data).any(axis=1)]
        processor.fit(col_data)
        process[col] = processor

        if col != "action":
            process[f"goal_{col}"] = process[col]

    # -- run evaluation
    policy = cfg.get("policy", "random")

    if policy != "random":
        model = swm.policy.AutoCostModel(cfg.policy)
        model = model.to("cuda")
        model = model.eval()
        model.requires_grad_(False)
        model.interpolate_pos_encoding = True
        config = swm.PlanConfig(**cfg.plan_config)
        solver = hydra.utils.instantiate(cfg.solver, model=model)
        policy = swm.policy.WorldModelPolicy(
            solver=solver, config=config, process=process, transform=transform
        )

    else:
        policy = swm.policy.RandomPolicy()

    results_path = (
        Path(swm.data.utils.get_cache_dir(), cfg.policy).parent
        if cfg.policy != "random"
        else Path(__file__).parent
    )

    # sample the episodes and the starting indices
    episode_len = get_episodes_length(dataset, ep_indices)
    max_start_idx = episode_len - cfg.eval.goal_offset_steps - 1
    max_start_idx_dict = {ep_id: max_start_idx[i] for i, ep_id in enumerate(ep_indices)}
    # Map each dataset row’s episode_idx to its max_start_idx
    col_name = "episode_idx" if "episode_idx" in dataset.column_names else "ep_idx"
    max_start_per_row = np.array(
        [max_start_idx_dict[ep_id] for ep_id in dataset.get_col_data(col_name)]
    )

    # remove all the lines of dataset for which dataset['step_idx'] > max_start_per_row
    valid_mask = dataset.get_col_data("step_idx") <= max_start_per_row
    valid_indices = np.nonzero(valid_mask)[0]
    print(valid_mask.sum(), "valid starting points found for evaluation.")

    g = np.random.default_rng(cfg.seed)
    random_episode_indices = g.choice(
        len(valid_indices) - 1, size=cfg.eval.num_eval, replace=False
    )

    # sort increasingly to avoid issues with HDF5Dataset indexing
    random_episode_indices = np.sort(valid_indices[random_episode_indices])

    print(random_episode_indices)

    eval_episodes = dataset.get_row_data(random_episode_indices)[col_name]
    eval_start_idx = dataset.get_row_data(random_episode_indices)["step_idx"]

    if len(eval_episodes) < cfg.eval.num_eval:
        raise ValueError("Not enough episodes with sufficient length for evaluation.")

    world.set_policy(policy)

    start_time = time.time()
    metrics = world.evaluate_from_dataset(
        dataset,
        start_steps=eval_start_idx.tolist(),
        goal_offset_steps=cfg.eval.goal_offset_steps,
        eval_budget=cfg.eval.eval_budget,
        episodes_idx=eval_episodes.tolist(),
        callables=OmegaConf.to_container(cfg.eval.get("callables"), resolve=True),
        video_path=results_path,
    )
    end_time = time.time()

    print(metrics)

    results_path = results_path / cfg.output.filename
    results_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    with results_path.open("a") as f:
        f.write("\n")  # separate from previous runs

        f.write("==== CONFIG ====\n")
        f.write(OmegaConf.to_yaml(cfg))
        f.write("\n")

        f.write("==== RESULTS ====\n")
        f.write(f"metrics: {metrics}\n")
        f.write(f"evaluation_time: {end_time - start_time} seconds\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()
```

### Assistant - 2026-04-23 22:35:20 EDT

````
是，**现在默认就是单进程单卡**。

直接证据有三处：

- [eval.py](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/eval.py:91) 里模型被硬编码到 `cuda`
  ```python
  model = model.to("cuda")
  ```
- [config/eval/solver/cem.yaml](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/config/eval/solver/cem.yaml:8) 和 [adam.yaml](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/config/eval/solver/adam.yaml:7) 里 `device: "cuda"`
- [config/eval/launcher/local.yaml](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/config/eval/launcher/local.yaml:5) 用的是 Hydra `basic` launcher，没有多进程/多机调度

不过它也不是完全串行。当前 eval 已经会把环境**在一张卡上批起来**：

- [config/eval/pusht.yaml](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/config/eval/pusht.yaml:8) 这类配置里  
  `world.num_envs: ${eval.num_eval}`

所以现在的模式更像是：

- 一份模型
- 一张 GPU
- 一个进程
- 多个 env batched rollout

**结论**
- 想做“单卡上更高吞吐”：现在已经在做一部分
- 想做“多卡并行 eval”：**可以做，但最方便的是多进程分片，不是把一个 `eval.py` 进程改成真正的多卡并行**

**为什么我说多进程分片最方便**
因为当前 `eval.py` 的结构很单一：

- 构造一个 `world`
- 构造一个 `policy`
- `world.evaluate_from_dataset(...)` 一次跑完

这套接口天然是“一个 policy / 一个 device / 一批 env”。  
如果想在**一个进程里同时用多张卡**，就得改：

- 模型副本管理
- solver device dispatch
- env 分组到不同 policy/world
- 汇总 metrics

这个改动不小。

**最实用的并行方案**
做成“多进程、多卡、按 episode shard 分片”：

1. 给 `eval.py` 加两个配置
- `eval.num_shards`
- `eval.shard_id`

2. 固定随机采样后，把 `random_episode_indices` 按 shard 切开

3. 每个进程只跑自己那份 episode

4. 每个进程指定自己的设备
- `solver.device=cuda:0`
- `solver.device=cuda:1`
- ...

5. 最后汇总结果文件

这样改动相对小，而且很稳。

**还有一个当前代码里的小问题**
即使你现在 override：
```bash
solver.device=cuda:1
```
模型本体还是会被 [eval.py](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/eval.py:91) 硬塞到 `"cuda"`，不完全跟随配置。  
所以至少应该先把它改成：

```python
device = cfg.solver.device
model = model.to(device)
```

这一步很小，但很必要。否则你连“手动指定单卡”都不干净。

**我的判断**
- “支持多卡并行 eval”是**方便做**的
- 但最合理的实现路径是：
  - 先把 device 从硬编码改成配置驱动
  - 再加 episode 分片
  - 用多个进程分别跑不同 GPU
- 不建议直接做单进程多卡

如果你要，我可以下一步直接把这套最小改动版实现出来：
- `eval.py` 支持 `solver.device`
- 支持 `eval.num_shards / eval.shard_id`
- 输出结果里带 shard 信息，方便汇总。
````

   123	def aggregate_shard_metrics(metrics_list):
   124	    if len(metrics_list) == 1:
   125	        return metrics_list[0]
   126	
   127	    merged = {}
   128	    counts = [len(np.asarray(m["episode_successes"])) for m in metrics_list]
   129	    merged["episode_successes"] = np.concatenate(
   130	        [np.asarray(m["episode_successes"]) for m in metrics_list], axis=0
   131	    )
   132	    merged["seeds"] = np.concatenate([np.asarray(m["seeds"]) for m in metrics_list], axis=0)
   133	    merged["success_rate"] = (
   134	        float(np.sum(merged["episode_successes"])) / len(merged["episode_successes"]) * 100.0
   135	    )
   136	
   137	    shared_keys = set.intersection(*(set(m.keys()) for m in metrics_list))
   138	    for key in sorted(shared_keys - {"episode_successes", "seeds", "success_rate"}):
   139	        values = [m[key] for m in metrics_list]
   140	        if all(isinstance(v, np.ndarray) for v in values):
   141	            if all(v.ndim >= 1 and len(v) == n for v, n in zip(values, counts)):
   142	                merged[key] = np.concatenate(values, axis=0)
   143	            elif all(v.ndim == 0 or v.size == 1 for v in values):
   144	                merged[key] = float(
   145	                    np.average([float(np.asarray(v)) for v in values], weights=counts)
   146	                )
   147	        elif all(np.isscalar(v) for v in values):
   148	            merged[key] = float(np.average(values, weights=counts))
   149	
   150	    return merged

   153	def run_eval_shard(cfg_dict, *, device: str, shard_id: int, episodes_idx, start_steps, video_root: str):
   154	    cfg = OmegaConf.create(cfg_dict)
   155	    cfg.world.max_episode_steps = 2 * cfg.eval.eval_budget
   156	    cfg.world.num_envs = len(episodes_idx)
   157	    if len(episodes_idx) == 0:
   158	        return {
   159	            "success_rate": 0.0,
   160	            "episode_successes": np.zeros(0, dtype=bool),
   161	            "seeds": np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int32),
   162	        }
   163	
   164	    if device.startswith("cuda"):
   165	        torch.cuda.set_device(torch.device(device))
   166

   168	    transform = {
   169	        "pixels": img_transform(cfg),
   170	        "goal": img_transform(cfg),
   171	    }
   172	    dataset = get_dataset(cfg, cfg.eval.dataset_name)
   173	    process = build_preprocessors(cfg, dataset)
   174	    policy = build_policy(cfg, process=process, transform=transform, device=device)
   175	    world.set_policy(policy)
   176	
   177	    metrics = world.evaluate_from_dataset(
   178	        dataset,
   179	        start_steps=np.asarray(start_steps).tolist(),
   180	        goal_offset_steps=cfg.eval.goal_offset_steps,
   181	        eval_budget=cfg.eval.eval_budget,
   182	        episodes_idx=np.asarray(episodes_idx).tolist(),
   183	        callables=OmegaConf.to_container(cfg.eval.get("callables"), resolve=True),
   184	        video_path=Path(video_root) / f"shard_{shard_id}",
   185	    )
   186	    world.close()
   187	    return metrics

   190	@hydra.main(version_base=None, config_path="./config/eval", config_name="pusht")
   191	def run(cfg: DictConfig):
   192	    """Run evaluation of dinowm vs random policy."""
   193	    assert (
   194	        cfg.plan_config.horizon * cfg.plan_config.action_block <= cfg.eval.eval_budget
   195	    ), "Planning horizon must be smaller than or equal to eval_budget"
   196	
   197	    # create world environment
   198	    cfg.world.max_episode_steps = 2 * cfg.eval.eval_budget
   199	    world = swm.World(**cfg.world, image_shape=(224, 224))
   200	
   201	    # create the transform
   202	    transform = {
   203	        "pixels": img_transform(cfg),
   204	        "goal": img_transform(cfg),
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    dataset = get_dataset(cfg, cfg.eval.dataset_name)
   208	    process = build_preprocessors(cfg, dataset)
   209	
   210	    # -- run evaluation
   211	    policy = cfg.get("policy", "random")
   212	
   213	    if policy != "random":
   214	        policy = build_policy(
   215	            cfg, process=process, transform=transform, device=cfg.solver.device
   216	        )
   217	
   218	    else:
   219	        policy = swm.policy.RandomPolicy()
   220	
   221	    results_path = (
   222	        Path(swm.data.utils.get_cache_dir(), cfg.policy).parent
   223	        if cfg.policy != "random"
   224	        else Path(__file__).parent
   225	    )
   226	
   227	    eval_episodes, eval_start_idx = sample_eval_starts(dataset, cfg)
   228	
   229	    world.set_policy(policy)
   230	
   231	    start_time = time.time()
   232	    visible_devices = get_visible_cuda_devices()
   233	    use_parallel = policy != "random" and len(visible_devices) > 1
   234	    if use_parallel:
   235	        shard_count = min(len(visible_devices), len(eval_episodes))
   236	        print(f"Using {shard_count} GPUs for evaluation: {visible_devices[:shard_count]}")
   237	        cfg_dict = OmegaConf.to_container(cfg, resolve=True)
   238	        episode_shards = np.array_split(eval_episodes, shard_count)
   239	        start_shards = np.array_split(eval_start_idx, shard_count)
   240	        ctx = mp.get_context("spawn")
   241	        shard_args = [
   242	            {
   243	                "cfg_dict": cfg_dict,
   244	                "device": visible_devices[i],
   245	                "shard_id": i,
   246	                "episodes_idx": episode_shards[i],
   247	                "start_steps": start_shards[i],
   248	                "video_root": str(results_path),
   249	            }
   250	            for i in range(shard_count)
   251	            if len(episode_shards[i]) > 0
   252	        ]
   253	        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(shard_args), mp_context=ctx) as executor:
   254	            metrics_list = list(
   255	                executor.map(lambda kwargs: run_eval_shard(**kwargs), shard_args)
   256	            )
   257	        metrics = aggregate_shard_metrics(metrics_list)
   258	    else:
   259	        if policy != "random":

+def aggregate_shard_metrics(metrics_list):
+    if len(metrics_list) == 1:
+        return metrics_list[0]
 
+    merged = {}
+    counts = [len(np.asarray(m["episode_successes"])) for m in metrics_list]
+    merged["episode_successes"] = np.concatenate(
+        [np.asarray(m["episode_successes"]) for m in metrics_list], axis=0
+    )
+    merged["seeds"] = np.concatenate([np.asarray(m["seeds"]) for m in metrics_list], axis=0)
+    merged["success_rate"] = (
+        float(np.sum(merged["episode_successes"])) / len(merged["episode_successes"]) * 100.0
+    )
+
+    shared_keys = set.intersection(*(set(m.keys()) for m in metrics_list))
+    for key in sorted(shared_keys - {"episode_successes", "seeds", "success_rate"}):
+        values = [m[key] for m in metrics_list]
+        if all(isinstance(v, np.ndarray) for v in values):
+            if all(v.ndim >= 1 and len(v) == n for v, n in zip(values, counts)):
+                merged[key] = np.concatenate(values, axis=0)
+            elif all(v.ndim == 0 or v.size == 1 for v in values):
+                merged[key] = float(
+                    np.average([float(np.asarray(v)) for v in values], weights=counts)
+                )
+        elif all(np.isscalar(v) for v in values):
+            merged[key] = float(np.average(values, weights=counts))
+
+    return merged

+def run(cfg: DictConfig):
+    """Run evaluation of dinowm vs random policy."""
+    assert (

+

+    start_time = time.time()
+    visible_devices = get_visible_cuda_devices()

+        print(f"Using {shard_count} GPUs for evaluation: {visible_devices[:shard_count]}")
+        cfg_dict = OmegaConf.to_container(cfg, resolve=True)
+        episode_shards = np.array_split(eval_episodes, shard_count)
+        start_shards = np.array_split(eval_start_idx, shard_count)
+        ctx = mp.get_context("spawn")

+                "shard_id": i,
+                "episodes_idx": episode_shards[i],
+                "start_steps": start_shards[i],

+            }
+            for i in range(shard_count)
+            if len(episode_shards[i]) > 0

+    else:

### Assistant - 2026-04-23 22:52:28 EDT

````
可以，我已经在本地把 [eval.py](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/eval.py:1) 改成按**当前执行环境可见的 GPU 数**自动决定并行度了。

现在的行为是：
- `policy=random`：仍然单进程
- `policy!=random` 且只看到 `0/1` 张 GPU：单进程单卡
- `policy!=random` 且看到多张 GPU：自动把 `eval_episodes` 按卡数分 shard，多进程分别跑在 `cuda:0/1/...` 上，最后在主进程汇总 `success_rate / episode_successes / seeds`

实现要点：
- 不再把模型硬编码到 `"cuda"`，而是每个 shard 跟随自己的 device
- 并行度直接用 `torch.cuda.device_count()`，所以会自动尊重 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES`
- 每个 shard 单独写视频到 `results_path/shard_k/`
- 主进程只负责采样 episode、分片、汇总结果

有一个边界要说明：
- 这次做的是**多卡 shard 并行**
- 每张卡内部的 solver 仍然受 `cfg.solver.batch_size` 控制  
  也就是说，如果 `batch_size=1`，单卡内还是按 env batch 顺序求解，只是现在不同 shard 会分到不同 GPU 上并行跑

静态检查已经过了：
```bash
python3 -m py_compile /home/ag/projects/le-wm/eval.py
```

目前这次改动还**没有提交推送**。如果你要，我下一步就直接提交并推到 3 个远端。

### User - 2026-04-23 23:45:58 EDT

### Assistant - 2026-04-23 23:46:48 EDT

```
能提速，但**不一定先靠多卡**。从代码结构看，我现在的判断是：

1. **模型规划这条链在 GPU 上是主要重算点**
- `eval.py` 里模型和 solver 都跑在 `cuda` 上：[eval.py](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/eval.py:69), [cem.yaml](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/config/eval/solver/cem.yaml:1)
- `stable-worldmodel` 的 `CEMSolver.solve()` 会反复调用 `model.get_cost(...)` 做候选动作评估，这部分是典型 GPU 负载  
  源码：https://raw.githubusercontent.com/galilai-group/stable-worldmodel/main/stable_worldmodel/solver/cem.py

2. **但你们当前单卡利用方式其实不理想**
- 现在 `world.num_envs = eval.num_eval`，环境是批起来的：[config/eval/pusht.yaml](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/config/eval/pusht.yaml:8)
- 可 `CEM` 配置里 `batch_size: 1`：[cem.yaml](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/config/eval/solver/cem.yaml:3)
- `CEMSolver` 内部会按 `for start_idx in range(0, total_envs, batch_size)` 分批处理 env，所以 `batch_size=1` 基本等于**逐个 env 串行规划**  
  源码：同上

这意味着：
- 你现在看起来像“500 个 env 并行 eval”
- 但 solver 这一层其实大概率还是**1 个 env 1 个 env 地在 GPU 上算**

所以多卡 shard **能提速**，因为它把这条串行链拆到多张卡上跑。  
但在很多情况下，**先把 `solver.batch_size` 从 `1` 调大**，单卡就可能先拿到很可观的提速。

3. **还有一个很可疑的 CPU / IO 瓶颈：你现在默认在保存视频**
- `eval.py` 调 `world.evaluate_from_dataset(...)` 时没有传 `save_video=False`：[eval.py](/home/ag/projects/le-wm/eval.py:144)
- `stable-worldmodel` 的 `evaluate_from_dataset` 默认 `save_video=True`，结束后会给每个 env 写 mp4  
  源码：https://raw.githubusercontent.com/galilai-group/stable-worldmodel/main/stable_worldmodel/world.py

如果你在跑 `num_eval=500`，那这里的**视频编码和磁盘写入**很可能已经是明显的 CPU/IO 开销，甚至可能比你想象的更夸张。

**所以我现在的结论是：**
- 真正的“算法计算瓶颈”大概率在 **GPU 侧的 CEM + world model cost evaluation**
- 但**当前 wall-clock 慢**，很可能同时被这两个问题拖住：
  - `solver.batch_size=1` 导致 GPU 侧规划串行化
  - 默认保存视频导致 CPU/IO 明显变慢

**优先级我建议这样排：**
1. 先关视频保存
2. 再把 `solver.batch_size` 往上调
3. 然后再上多卡 shard 并行

因为：
- 关视频通常是立竿见影的
- 调大 `batch_size` 比多卡改动更小
- 多卡更像在单卡已经吃满后，再继续扩吞吐

**如果你想快速判断瓶颈在哪**
- 看 `nvidia-smi` / GPU util
- 如果 GPU 利用率很低，但 eval 还是慢，通常是 CPU/IO 或 batch 太小
- 如果 GPU 利用率高，且日志里 `CEM solve time` 占大头，那就是 planner/model 路径在卡

如果你要，我下一步建议直接做两件小改动：
- 给 `eval.py` 加 `save_video` 开关，默认关
- 把 `solver.batch_size` 暴露成更容易 override 的常用参数，并给你一个推荐 sweep 范围。
```

+def aggregate_shard_metrics(metrics_list):
+    if len(metrics_list) == 1:
+        return metrics_list[0]
 
+    merged = {}
+    counts = [len(np.asarray(m["episode_successes"])) for m in metrics_list]
+    merged["episode_successes"] = np.concatenate(
+        [np.asarray(m["episode_successes"]) for m in metrics_list], axis=0
+    )
+    merged["seeds"] = np.concatenate([np.asarray(m["seeds"]) for m in metrics_list], axis=0)
+    merged["success_rate"] = (
+        float(np.sum(merged["episode_successes"])) / len(merged["episode_successes"]) * 100.0
+    )
+
+    shared_keys = set.intersection(*(set(m.keys()) for m in metrics_list))
+    for key in sorted(shared_keys - {"episode_successes", "seeds", "success_rate"}):
+        values = [m[key] for m in metrics_list]
+        if all(isinstance(v, np.ndarray) for v in values):
+            if all(v.ndim >= 1 and len(v) == n for v, n in zip(values, counts)):
+                merged[key] = np.concatenate(values, axis=0)
+            elif all(v.ndim == 0 or v.size == 1 for v in values):
+                merged[key] = float(
+                    np.average([float(np.asarray(v)) for v in values], weights=counts)
+                )
+        elif all(np.isscalar(v) for v in values):
+            merged[key] = float(np.average(values, weights=counts))
+
+    return merged

import os

os.environ["MUJOCO_GL"] = "egl"

import time
from pathlib import Path

import hydra
import numpy as np
import stable_pretraining as spt
import torch
from omegaconf import DictConfig, OmegaConf
from sklearn import preprocessing
from torchvision.transforms import v2 as transforms
import stable_worldmodel as swm


def img_transform(cfg):
    transform = transforms.Compose(
        [
            transforms.ToImage(),
            transforms.ToDtype(torch.float32, scale=True),
            transforms.Normalize(**spt.data.dataset_stats.ImageNet),
            transforms.Resize(size=cfg.eval.img_size),
        ]
    )
    return transform


def get_episodes_length(dataset, episodes):
    col_name = "episode_idx" if "episode_idx" in dataset.column_names else "ep_idx"

    episode_idx = dataset.get_col_data(col_name)
    step_idx = dataset.get_col_data("step_idx")
    lengths = []
    for ep_id in episodes:
        lengths.append(np.max(step_idx[episode_idx == ep_id]) + 1)
    return np.array(lengths)


def get_dataset(cfg, dataset_name):
    dataset_path = Path(cfg.cache_dir or swm.data.utils.get_cache_dir())
    dataset = swm.data.HDF5Dataset(
        dataset_name,
        keys_to_cache=cfg.dataset.keys_to_cache,
        cache_dir=dataset_path,
    )
    return dataset


@hydra.main(version_base=None, config_path="./config/eval", config_name="pusht")
def run(cfg: DictConfig):
    """Run evaluation of dinowm vs random policy."""
    assert (
        cfg.plan_config.horizon * cfg.plan_config.action_block <= cfg.eval.eval_budget
    ), "Planning horizon must be smaller than or equal to eval_budget"

    # create world environment
    cfg.world.max_episode_steps = 2 * cfg.eval.eval_budget
    world = swm.World(**cfg.world, image_shape=(224, 224))

    # create the transform
    transform = {
        "pixels": img_transform(cfg),
        "goal": img_transform(cfg),
    }

    dataset = get_dataset(cfg, cfg.eval.dataset_name)
    stats_dataset = dataset  # get_dataset(cfg, cfg.dataset.stats)
    col_name = "episode_idx" if "episode_idx" in dataset.column_names else "ep_idx"
    ep_indices, _ = np.unique(stats_dataset.get_col_data(col_name), return_index=True)

    process = {}
    for col in cfg.dataset.keys_to_cache:
        if col in ["pixels"]:
            continue
        processor = preprocessing.StandardScaler()
        col_data = stats_dataset.get_col_data(col)
        col_data = col_data[~np.isnan(col_data).any(axis=1)]
        processor.fit(col_data)
        process[col] = processor

        if col != "action":
            process[f"goal_{col}"] = process[col]

    # -- run evaluation
    policy = cfg.get("policy", "random")

    if policy != "random":
        model = swm.policy.AutoCostModel(cfg.policy)
        model = model.to("cuda")
        model = model.eval()
        model.requires_grad_(False)
        model.interpolate_pos_encoding = True
        config = swm.PlanConfig(**cfg.plan_config)
        solver = hydra.utils.instantiate(cfg.solver, model=model)
        policy = swm.policy.WorldModelPolicy(
            solver=solver, config=config, process=process, transform=transform
        )

    else:
        policy = swm.policy.RandomPolicy()

    results_path = (
        Path(swm.data.utils.get_cache_dir(), cfg.policy).parent
        if cfg.policy != "random"
        else Path(__file__).parent
    )

    # sample the episodes and the starting indices
    episode_len = get_episodes_length(dataset, ep_indices)
    max_start_idx = episode_len - cfg.eval.goal_offset_steps - 1
    max_start_idx_dict = {ep_id: max_start_idx[i] for i, ep_id in enumerate(ep_indices)}
    # Map each dataset row’s episode_idx to its max_start_idx
    col_name = "episode_idx" if "episode_idx" in dataset.column_names else "ep_idx"
    max_start_per_row = np.array(
        [max_start_idx_dict[ep_id] for ep_id in dataset.get_col_data(col_name)]
    )

    # remove all the lines of dataset for which dataset['step_idx'] > max_start_per_row
    valid_mask = dataset.get_col_data("step_idx") <= max_start_per_row
    valid_indices = np.nonzero(valid_mask)[0]
    print(valid_mask.sum(), "valid starting points found for evaluation.")

    g = np.random.default_rng(cfg.seed)
    random_episode_indices = g.choice(
        len(valid_indices) - 1, size=cfg.eval.num_eval, replace=False
    )

    # sort increasingly to avoid issues with HDF5Dataset indexing
    random_episode_indices = np.sort(valid_indices[random_episode_indices])

    print(random_episode_indices)

    eval_episodes = dataset.get_row_data(random_episode_indices)[col_name]
    eval_start_idx = dataset.get_row_data(random_episode_indices)["step_idx"]

    if len(eval_episodes) < cfg.eval.num_eval:
        raise ValueError("Not enough episodes with sufficient length for evaluation.")

    world.set_policy(policy)

    start_time = time.time()
    metrics = world.evaluate_from_dataset(
        dataset,
        start_steps=eval_start_idx.tolist(),
        goal_offset_steps=cfg.eval.goal_offset_steps,
        eval_budget=cfg.eval.eval_budget,
        episodes_idx=eval_episodes.tolist(),
        callables=OmegaConf.to_container(cfg.eval.get("callables"), resolve=True),
        video_path=results_path,
    )
    end_time = time.time()

    print(metrics)

    results_path = results_path / cfg.output.filename
    results_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    with results_path.open("a") as f:
        f.write("\n")  # separate from previous runs

        f.write("==== CONFIG ====\n")
        f.write(OmegaConf.to_yaml(cfg))
        f.write("\n")

        f.write("==== RESULTS ====\n")
        f.write(f"metrics: {metrics}\n")
        f.write(f"evaluation_time: {end_time - start_time} seconds\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()
